+const fullRevision='18446744073709551615';
+function setup({ids=[0],rev='18',mutate=row=>row,delay=null}={}) {
+  const context={id:'project'},frame={context},edit={bid:1},focus={t:'face',id:1},calls=[],listeners={},project={revision:2};
+  const row=id=>mutate({id,eligible:true,reason:null,owner:null,groups:[],groupsKnown:true,placed:true,placementOrder:String(id+1),
+    definition:{A:{mode:'source',source:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:0,fieldId:2},override:null},
+      B:{mode:'source',source:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:0,fieldId:7},override:{kind:'literal',value:33}},
+      role:'boundary',priority:1,placement:{on:0,anchor:'at-face e0',selector:'',direction:'+u',side:'+n'}},effective:{A:27,B:33}});
+  const workspace={context:()=>context,history:{frame},getProjectState:()=>project,assertEditable:()=>{},escape:String};
+  const fe={timberSelection:new Set(ids),sel:ids.length===1?{kind:'timber',id:ids[0]}:null,tool:'select',panX:0,zoom:1};
+  const oc={edit,focus,busy:false},connection={session:'session-1',phase:'ready'};
+  const sandbox={structuredClone,console,setTimeout,clearTimeout,AbortSignal,BigInt,ReferenceWorkspace:workspace,fe,oc,connection,
+    feSid:()=>0,pageRuntimeVersion:'test-version',checkApiSession:()=>{},toast:()=>{},
+    document:{getElementById:()=>null,activeElement:null,addEventListener:(name,handler)=>{(listeners[name]??=[]).push(handler);}},
+    FaceContextMenu:{refreshEligibility:()=>{}},
+    apiRequest:async command=>{calls.push(command);assert.equal(command,'engineering-read');return {ok:true,revision:Number(rev),state:{materials:[],specs:[],applications:[]}};},
+    fetch:async (url,options)=>{
+      assert.equal(url,'/timber-edit/read');const body=JSON.parse(options.body);calls.push(body);
+      if(delay)await delay();
+      return {ok:true,status:200,json:async()=>({ok:true,schema:'timber-edit-read-result/1',revision:rev,
+        contextKey:'a'.repeat(64),boundaryId:1,maxOperations:64,eligible:true,reason:null,
+        timbers:body.timberIds.map(row),recipeKey:'b'.repeat(64)})};
+    }};
+  sandbox.window={DraftGate:{register:()=>{}},addEventListener:(name,handler)=>{(listeners[name]??=[]).push(handler);}};
+  vm.createContext(sandbox);vm.runInContext(source,sandbox,{filename:'timber-edit.js'});
+  return {adapter:sandbox.window.TimberEdit,workspace,project,fe,oc,connection,calls,listeners};
+}
+test('R11-UI-U64: catalog numbers never round the exact timber revision',async()=>{
+  const s=setup({rev:fullRevision});await assert.doesNotReject(s.adapter.copy());
+  assert.equal(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,'');
+  assert.deepEqual(s.calls.map(item=>typeof item==='string'?item:item.schema),['timber-edit-read/1','engineering-read','timber-edit-read/1']);
+  assert.equal(s.calls[0].timberIds[0],0);assert.equal(s.project.revision,2);
+});
+test('R11-UI-ELIGIBILITY: owner, groups and unknown qualification reject the whole copy',async()=>{
+  for(const mutate of [row=>({...row,owner:{kind:'assembly-instance',id:0}}),row=>({...row,groups:[{kind:'boundary-pair',id:335}]}),row=>({...row,groupsKnown:false})]){
+    const s=setup({mutate});await assert.rejects(s.adapter.copy(),/資格|群組|所有權/);
+    assert.notEqual(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,'');
+  }
+});
+test('R11-UI-PRIORITY: full int64 text is authoritative and unsafe legacy projections are rejected',async()=>{
+  const maximum='9223372036854775807';
+  const s=setup({mutate:row=>({...row,definition:{...row.definition,role:'custom',priority:Number(maximum),priorityText:maximum}})});
+  await assert.doesNotReject(s.adapter.copy());assert.equal(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,'');
+  const unsafe=setup({mutate:row=>({...row,definition:{...row.definition,role:'custom',priority:Number(maximum)}})});
+  await assert.rejects(unsafe.adapter.copy(),/優先序/);
+  const invalid=setup({mutate:row=>({...row,definition:{...row.definition,priorityText:'01'}})});
+  await assert.rejects(invalid.adapter.copy(),/優先序/);
+});
+test('R11-UI-ORDER: a late read cannot authorize a reordered selection',async()=>{
+  let release;const wait=new Promise(resolve=>{release=resolve;});
+  const s=setup({ids:[0,7],delay:()=>wait}),request=s.adapter.prepareMenu();
+  s.fe.timberSelection=new Set([7,0]);release();await request;
+  assert.match(s.adapter.menuItems().batch,/正在核對/);
+});
+test('R11-UI-RECIPE-LIFETIME: pan and zoom preserve recipes, frame and revision changes expire them',async()=>{
+  const s=setup();await s.adapter.copy();s.fe.panX=80;s.fe.zoom=1.5;
+  assert.equal(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,'');
+  s.project.revision++;assert.match(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,/世代/);
+  s.project.revision--;s.workspace.history.frame={context:s.workspace.context()};
+  assert.match(s.adapter.menuItems().paste,/世代/);
+});
+test('R11-UI-INPUT-IME: text and composition shortcuts never reach geometry copy',async()=>{
+  const s=setup(),key=s.listeners.keydown[0];
+  const event=extra=>({key:'c',ctrlKey:true,metaKey:false,altKey:false,keyCode:67,isComposing:false,
+    target:{isContentEditable:false,closest:selector=>selector==='#faceEditorViewport'?{}:null},
+    preventDefault:()=>assert.fail('text key was intercepted'),stopImmediatePropagation:()=>assert.fail('text event was stopped'),...extra});
+  key(event({isComposing:true}));
+  key(event({target:{isContentEditable:true,closest:()=>null}}));
+  key(event({target:{isContentEditable:false,closest:selector=>selector.includes('input,textarea')?{}:null}}));
+  for(const handler of s.listeners.compositionstart)handler();key(event());
+  let stopped=0;
+  key(event({key:'Escape',target:{closest:selector=>selector==='#timberEditForm'?{}:null},stopPropagation:()=>{stopped++;}}));
+  assert.equal(stopped,1,'composition Escape must not reach the global drawer handler');
+  await Promise.resolve();assert.equal(s.calls.length,0);
+});
diff --git a/tests/run_timber_edit_ui_mutations.py b/tests/run_timber_edit_ui_mutations.py
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/run_timber_edit_ui_mutations.py
@@ -0,0 +1,84 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Execute named adapter mutations on isolated files; never edit the worktree.
+
+This is a Node VM boundary check. Native and real Chrome mutation gates remain
+separate. Every mutant must be syntactically valid, fail its named assertion,
+and pass again with the identical original bytes restored.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+
+
+def sha(data):
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+    source = root / 'geometry-web/timber-edit.js'
+    tests = root / 'tests/test_timber_edit_ui.js'
+    definitions = root / 'tests/timber_edit_ui_mutations.json'
+    original = source.read_bytes()
+    if args.output and args.output.exists():
+        parser.error('--output must not already exist; previous receipts are immutable')
+    output = args.output or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='r11-ui-mutations-'))
+    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=args.output is None)
+    command = ['node', '--test-reporter=tap', str(tests)]
+    receipt = {'kind': 'adapter-functions', 'source_sha256': sha(original),
+               'test_sha256': sha(tests.read_bytes()), 'mutations_sha256': sha(definitions.read_bytes()),
+               'runner_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()), 'command': command, 'mutations': []}
+    try:
+        for definition in json.loads(definitions.read_text(encoding='utf-8')):
+            case = output / definition['name']
+            target = case / 'source/geometry-web/timber-edit.js'
+            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+            anchor = definition['anchor'].encode('utf-8')
+            assert original.count(anchor) == 1, definition['name'] + ': anchor must occur once'
+            mutant = original.replace(anchor, definition['replacement'].encode('utf-8'))
+            env = dict(os.environ, GEOMETRY_TIMBER_UI_ROOT=str(target.parent.parent))
+            record = {'name': definition['name'], 'case': definition['case'], 'runs': []}
+            receipt['mutations'].append(record)
+            baseline_count = None
+            for phase, data in [('baseline', original), ('mutant', mutant), ('restored', original)]:
+                target.write_bytes(data)
+                syntax = subprocess.run(['node', '--check', str(target)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
+                assert syntax.returncode == 0, definition['name'] + ': invalid syntax is not a kill'
+                run = subprocess.run(command, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
+                (case / (phase + '.log')).write_bytes((run.stdout + run.stderr).encode('utf-8'))
+                summary = {key: int(count) for key, count in re.findall(r'^# (tests|pass|fail|cancelled|skipped) (\d+)$', run.stdout, re.M)}
+                failures = re.findall(r'^not ok \d+ - ([^\n]+)\n(.*?)(?=^# Subtest:|^\d+\.\.\d+|\Z)', run.stdout, re.M | re.S)
+                record['runs'].append({'phase': phase, 'exit_code': run.returncode, 'source_sha256': sha(data), 'syntax_exit': syntax.returncode, 'summary': summary})
+                assert summary.get('cancelled') == 0 and summary.get('skipped') == 0, definition['name'] + ': cancelled/skipped cases cannot count'
+                if phase == 'mutant':
+                    assert run.returncode == 1 and len(failures) == 1 and failures[0][0].startswith(definition['case'] + ':'), definition['name'] + ': only the named case may fail'
+                    assert re.search(r"^\s+code: ['\"]ERR_ASSERTION['\"]$", failures[0][1], re.M), definition['name'] + ': named failure was not an assertion'
+                    assert summary.get('fail') == 1 and summary.get('tests') == baseline_count and summary.get('pass') == baseline_count - 1, definition['name'] + ': all other cases must pass'
+                else:
+                    assert run.returncode == 0 and not failures and summary.get('fail') == 0 and summary.get('pass', 0) > 0 and summary.get('tests') == summary.get('pass'), definition['name'] + ': baseline/restore failed'
+                    if baseline_count is None:
+                        baseline_count = summary['tests']
+                    else:
+                        assert summary['tests'] == baseline_count, definition['name'] + ': restored case count changed'
+            record['status'] = 'killed-and-restored'
+        assert source.read_bytes() == original, 'worktree source was modified'
+        receipt['status'] = 'pass'
+    except BaseException as error:
+        receipt['status'] = 'failed'
+        receipt['error'] = str(error)
+        raise
+    finally:
+        (output / 'receipt.json').write_bytes((json.dumps(receipt, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n').encode('utf-8'))
+        print(output / 'receipt.json')
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main()
diff --git a/tests/timber_edit_ui_mutations.json b/tests/timber_edit_ui_mutations.json
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/timber_edit_ui_mutations.json
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+[
+  {
+    "name": "R11-UI-M01-catalog-rounding",
+    "case": "R11-UI-U64",
+    "anchor": "after.revision !== before.revision",
+    "replacement": "String(catalog.revision) !== before.revision"
+  },
+  {
+    "name": "R11-UI-M02-priority-rounding",
+    "case": "R11-UI-PRIORITY",
+    "anchor": "const text = definition?.priorityText ?? (Number.isSafeInteger(definition?.priority) && definition.priority > 0 ? String(definition.priority) : null);",
+    "replacement": "const text = String(definition?.priority);"
+  },
+  {
+    "name": "R11-UI-M03-composition-escape-bubbles",
+    "case": "R11-UI-INPUT-IME",
+    "anchor": "if(event.target.closest?.('#timberEditForm'))event.stopPropagation();",
+    "replacement": "/* withdrawn: composition Escape reaches the global geometry handler */"
+  }
+]

@@ -0,0 +1,336 @@
+{
+  "schema": "geometry-browser-mutations/1",
+  "suite": "timber-reuse-116",
+  "scope": "GEOMETRY_TIMBER_REUSE_ONLY",
+  "caseFilter": "GEOMETRY_TIMBER_REUSE_CASE",
+  "resultPrefix": "R116_CASE_RESULT ",
+  "cases": [
+    "C01",
+    "C02",
+    "C03",
+    "C04",
+    "C05",
+    "C06",
+    "C07",
+    "C08",
+    "C09",
+    "C10",
+    "C11",
+    "C12"
+  ],
+  "inputs": [
+    "geometry-web/cdp_test.js",
+    "geometry-web/timber_reuse_test.js",
+    "geometry-web/timber_reuse_mutations.json",
+    "geometry-web/browser_mutation_check.py",
+    "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+    "geometry-web/timber_edit_bridge.inc",
+    "geometry-web/timber_edit_transport.py"
+  ],
+  "mutations": [
+    {
+      "id": "J01-unwire-copy-entry",
+      "case": "C01",
+      "assertions": [
+        "REUSE_ACTION_READY"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C01",
+        "C11"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/face-context-menu.js",
+          "before": "if (a.act==='timber-copy') await TimberEdit.copy();",
+          "after": "if (a.act==='timber-copy') toast('mutation: entry disconnected');"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J02-unwire-eligibility-refresh",
+      "case": "C02",
+      "assertions": [
+        "SINGLE_EDIT_READY"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C02",
+        "C11"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "menuRead = {ids,stamp,result}; FaceContextMenu.refreshEligibility();",
+          "after": "menuRead = {ids,stamp,result}; FaceContextMenu.sync();"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J03-bake-recipe-source-display",
+      "case": "C02",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C02_RECIPE_SHOWS_EXACT_TYPED_FIELDS"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C01",
+        "C02"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "const cloneRecipe = value => ({...value,definition:clone(value.definition),effective:clone(value.effective)});",
+          "after": "const cloneRecipe = value => ({...value,definition:{...clone(value.definition),A:{mode:'direct',value:value.effective.A},B:{mode:'direct',value:value.effective.B}},effective:clone(value.effective)});"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J04-collapse-equal-effective-sources",
+      "case": "C02",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C02_EQUAL_NUMBERS_DIFFERENT_SOURCE_MARKED_MIXED"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C02",
+        "C03"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "    const definitions = value.read.timbers.map(row => field === 'direction' ? row.definition.placement.direction : field === 'priority' ? priorityText(row.definition) : row.definition[field]);",
+          "after": "    const definitions = value.read.timbers.map(row => field === 'direction' ? row.definition.placement.direction : field === 'priority' ? priorityText(row.definition) : ['A','B'].includes(field) ? row.effective[field] : row.definition[field]);"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J05-default-all-fields-to-change",
+      "case": "C03",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C03_KEEP_EACH_ORIGINAL_DEFAULT"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C03",
+        "C04"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "<input type=\"checkbox\" data-timber-patch=\"${field}\">",
+          "after": "<input type=\"checkbox\" data-timber-patch=\"${field}\" checked>"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J06-filter-selection-to-first",
+      "case": "C04",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C04_FINAL_PAIR_PREVIEW_RESPONSE",
+        "C04_ALL_EXPLICIT_PATCHES_ONE_CANDIDATE"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C04",
+        "C10"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "return value.ids.map(id=>({op:'update-timber-fields',id,fields:clone(fields)}));",
+          "after": "return value.ids.slice(0,1).map(id=>({op:'update-timber-fields',id,fields:clone(fields)}));"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J07-trust-unknown-groups",
+      "case": "C05",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C05_UNKNOWN_WHOLE_SELECTION_DISABLED"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C05"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "row.owner !== null || row.groupsKnown !== true ||",
+          "after": "row.owner !== null ||"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J08-ignore-group-membership",
+      "case": "C05",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C05_GROUP_WHOLE_SELECTION_DISABLED"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C05"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "!Array.isArray(row.groups) || row.groups.length || row.placed !== true",
+          "after": "!Array.isArray(row.groups) || row.placed !== true"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J09-ignore-owner-row",
+      "case": "C05",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C05_OWNER_WHOLE_SELECTION_DISABLED"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C05"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "row.eligible !== true || row.owner !== null || row.groupsKnown !== true",
+          "after": "row.eligible !== true || row.groupsKnown !== true"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J10-ignore-selection-order",
+      "case": "C06",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C06_LATE_REPLY_CANNOT_REOPEN_CONFIRM"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C06",
+        "C09"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "const selectionCurrent = stamp => stamp.selection === JSON.stringify(selectedIds()) && stamp.selected === JSON.stringify(fe.sel) && stamp.tool === fe.tool;",
+          "after": "const selectionCurrent = stamp => stamp.selected === JSON.stringify(fe.sel) && stamp.tool === fe.tool;"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J11-remove-final-visible-input-check",
+      "case": "C09",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C09_FINAL_RAW_FORM_GUARD"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C06",
+        "C09"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {
+          "file": "geometry-web/timber-edit.js",
+          "before": "if (value.previewInput !== rawForm()) {changed(value);throw Error('輸入已變更，請重新預覽。');}",
+          "after": "/* mutation: final visible-input check removed */"
+        }
+      ]
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "J12-dispatch-shortcuts-during-composition",
+      "case": "C08",
+      "assertions": [
+        "C08_COMPOSING_ESCAPE_LEAVES_FORM"
+      ],
+      "requirements": [
+        "C08"
+      ],
+      "changes": [
+        {

@@ -0,0 +1,79 @@
+/* R11 second slice A / issue #116: real menu, same-face batch, native candidates.
+ * Run only through cdp_test.js. Native fixture setup is labelled separately from
+ * trusted UI actions. Fault injection holds/drops actual completed responses;
+ * it never manufactures a successful native result.
+ */
+'use strict';
+const fs=require('node:fs'),os=require('node:os'),path=require('node:path');
+const {spawn}=require('node:child_process'),net=require('node:net'),crypto=require('node:crypto');
+const {isDeepStrictEqual:same}=require('node:util');
+const cdp=require('./cdp_helpers.js');
+const P=require('./project-store.js'),zip=require('./vendor/fflate/umd/index.js');
+const CASE_IDS=Object.freeze(Array.from({length:12},(_,i)=>'C'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')));
+const READ_COMMAND=/^(?:dump|objects|surfaces|references|engineering-read|section-profile-read|assembly-read|source-inspect|render|boundary \d+|obstacles \d+)$/;
+const ZERO_FIXTURE=[
+  'boundary 1 outline 0,0 4000,0 4000,3000 0,3000',
+  'placement 0 on boundary 1 origin 0 0 2500 u +x v +y',
+  'timber 0 27 33 role boundary','place 0 on 0 at-face e0 +u +n'
+];
+const PAIR_FIXTURE=[...ZERO_FIXTURE,'timber 7 29 33 role boundary','place 7 on 0 at-face e2 +u +n'];
+const ATOMIC_FIXTURE=[...ZERO_FIXTURE.slice(0,2),
+  'spec position1 500','spec position2 550','guide 1 0 v spec:position1','guide 2 0 v spec:position2',
+  'timber 90 20 20 role boundary','place 90 on 0 at-face e0 +u +n',
+  'timber 91 20 20 role boundary','place 91 on 0 at-face e2 +u +n',
+  'timber 1 20 33 role primary','place 1 on 0 at-line guide 1 +v +n',
+  'timber 2 80 33 role primary','place 2 on 0 at-line guide 2 +v +n'];
+const REPEAT_FIXTURE=[...ZERO_FIXTURE,'spec position1 500','spec position2 550',
+  'guide 1 0 v spec:position1','guide 2 0 v spec:position2',
+  'timber 90 20 20 role boundary','place 90 on 0 at-face e2 +u +n',
+  'timber 1 20 33 role primary','place 1 on 0 at-line guide 1 +v +n',
+  'timber 2 80 33 role primary','place 2 on 0 at-line guide 2 +v +n',
+  'spec repeat3 1000','spec repeat4 1500','guide 3 0 v spec:repeat3','guide 4 0 v spec:repeat4'];
+const SOURCE_A={mode:'source',source:{kind:'parameter',parameterId:10,fieldId:5},override:{kind:'literal',value:27}};
+const SOURCE_B={mode:'source',source:{kind:'material',id:'pine',version:1,field:'B'},override:null};
+const sha256=bytes=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+function selectCases(value){
+  if(value===undefined)return [...CASE_IDS];
+  const ids=typeof value==='string'?value.split(',').map(x=>x.trim()):[];
+  if(!ids.length||new Set(ids).size!==ids.length||ids.some(id=>!CASE_IDS.includes(id)))throw Error('GEOMETRY_TIMBER_REUSE_CASE requires unique C01..C12 IDs');
+  return ids;
+}
+function fault(kind,assertion,message,detail){const e=new Error(message+(detail===undefined?'':' '+JSON.stringify(detail)));e.r116={kind,assertion};return e;}
+function installProbe(){
+  if(window.__r116)throw Error('R116 probe already installed');
+  const original=window.fetch,p=window.__r116={original,rows:[],events:[],pending:0,gate:null};
+  const observe=e=>p.events.push({type:e.type,trusted:e.isTrusted,key:e.key,composing:e.isComposing,ctrl:e.ctrlKey,meta:e.metaKey,target:e.target?.id||e.target?.tagName});
+  for(const type of ['contextmenu','keydown','keyup','compositionstart','compositionend'])document.addEventListener(type,observe,true);
+  p.arm=(pathname,command='',mode='hold',alter=null)=>{
+    if(p.gate)throw Error('R116 gate already armed');
+    let release;p.gate={pathname,command,mode,alter,phase:'armed',claims:0,promise:new Promise(r=>release=r),release:()=>release()};
+  };
+  const wrapped=async(...args)=>{
+    const url=new URL(typeof args[0]==='string'?args[0]:args[0].url,location.href);
+    if(url.origin!==location.origin||!['/api','/timber-edit/read','/timber-edit/preview'].includes(url.pathname))return original.apply(window,args);
+    let body;try{body=JSON.parse(args[1]?.body||'{}');}catch(_){return original.apply(window,args);}
+    const row={path:url.pathname,command:body.cmd||'',body,settled:false};p.rows.push(row);p.pending++;
+    try{
+      if(body.receipt&&p.blockReceipts)throw new TypeError('R116 injected receipt temporarily unavailable');
+      const response=await original.apply(window,args);let data;
+      try{data=await response.clone().json();row.result=data;row.status=response.status;}catch(e){row.observationError=String(e);}
+      const gate=p.gate;
+      if(gate?.phase==='armed'&&gate.pathname===url.pathname&&(!gate.command||row.command.startsWith(gate.command))){
+        gate.phase='held';gate.claims++;gate.nativeOK=data?.ok===true;
+        if(gate.mode==='hold')await gate.promise;
+        gate.phase='released';
+        if(gate.mode==='drop')throw new TypeError('R116 injected lost reply after native completion');
+        if(gate.mode==='eligibility'){
+          const changed=structuredClone(data);
+          for(const timber of changed.timbers||[])if(gate.alter==='unknown')timber.groupsKnown=false;
+          else if(gate.alter==='group')timber.groups=[{kind:'boundary-pair',id:335}];
+          else if(gate.alter==='owner')timber.owner={kind:'assembly-instance',id:116};
+          return new Response(JSON.stringify(changed),{status:response.status,headers:response.headers});
+        }
+      }
+      return response;
+    }catch(e){row.error=String(e);throw e;}finally{row.settled=true;p.pending--;}
+  };
+  window.fetch=wrapped;
+  p.cleanup=()=>{p.gate?.release();if(window.fetch===wrapped)window.fetch=original;for(const type of ['contextmenu','keydown','keyup','compositionstart','compositionend'])document.removeEventListener(type,observe,true);delete window.__r116;};
+}

+  const dump=async()=>(await query('dump')).text;

+  const mark=()=>page(()=>window.__r116.rows.length);

+  const fill=cdp.makeFill({ctl,key,send,sleep},{afterMs:50});

+    need('SELECT_OPTION_AVAILABLE',index>=0,{selector,value});await ctl(selector);await key('Home','Home',36);

+  const reuseItem=name=>'#feCtxMenu [data-fe-act="timber-'+name+'"]';

+    await ctl(reuseItem(action));await settled();

+  const batch=async ids=>{await selectTimbers(ids);await wait('BATCH_FORM_VISIBLE',"document.getElementById('timberEditForm')?.dataset.mode==='batch'");check('BATCH_RIGHT_PANE',await page(()=>!!document.getElementById('faceInspectorContent').querySelector('#timberEditForm')));};
+  const reopenBatchFromMenu=async ids=>{

+    await ctl('#feCtxMenu [data-fe-act="timber-batch"]');await settled();await wait('BATCH_FORM_REOPENED_FROM_MENU',"document.getElementById('timberEditForm')?.dataset.mode==='batch'");
+  };

+  const explicit=async(field,value)=>{

+  if(results.length!==selected.length||results.some(r=>r.status==='error'))throw Error('R116 incomplete cases: '+results.filter(r=>r.status!=='pass').map(r=>r.id+'/'+r.assertion).join(','));
+};
+module.exports.caseIds=CASE_IDS;module.exports.selectCases=selectCases;
```

## 【ChatGPT 實作】R11 第二片 — 4/6（補丁 P：原生候選與傳輸）

基底：`feat/116-object-canvas-menu-20261027 @ 5c5700b5c151390f8f5c22a74655ee446ecfd2f0`。交付時間：2026-10-29 14:42 Asia/Taipei。

本片：`04-R11-P-native.patch`；UTF-8、LF；**54,733 bytes**。

**SHA-256：`c76fc71e26b45d668bdb5118af2bf710790e18b18b73b903b012fad1232f250b`**

改動檔清單：

- `geometry-core/transactions.inc`
- `geometry-web/engineering_bridge.inc`
- `geometry-web/reference_bridge.inc`
- `geometry-web/web_bridge.c`
- `geometry-web/serve.py`
- `geometry-web/timber_edit_bridge.inc`
- `geometry-web/timber_edit_transport.py`
- `geometry-web/docs/H-116-TIMBER-1.md`

## 原生 P 的行為

新增 H 層 typed read／preview／confirm／cancel adapter 與嚴格 JSON 傳輸；UI 不計算幾何或烘焙來源。沿用既有候選、工程來源、放置及共同驗證，不新增 K/W 幾何規則。

批改先檢查整組適用性，把全部明選定義寫入同一候選，再共同驗證一次；成功確認只發布一次、記一次 history。保留原 id／record slot／放置順序；明選 direct 只解除該欄自己的來源，省略欄位保持原定義。同值不同來源是變更，完整定義相同則 no-op、token=null。

複製以原生 opaque recipeKey 保存 typed A/B、override、role／priority，綁定專案 context、完整 revision 及同面。預覽使用私有 `new:0` handle，確認成功才正式配號；0 合法、uint32 回繞跳過既有 id。配方、repeat 與未確認候選均不持久化。

revision 採 canonical uint64 字串；priorityText 採 canonical 正 int64 字串，保留 9007199254740993 及 int64 上限，不套尺寸座標上限。請求上限 1 MiB、64 個批改目標；未知欄位、重複目標、非法錨點、跨面及不適用物件整筆拒絕。

不同 token family 不能借用確認或互相取消。確認走既有 requestId／receipt 流程，接線在 6/6；掉回覆只能查原 receipt，不重送。

契約與固定基底限制詳本片 `geometry-web/docs/H-116-TIMBER-1.md`。三個新 adapter 的 H 登記集中在 6/6。

diff --git a/geometry-web/timber_edit_bridge.inc b/geometry-web/timber_edit_bridge.inc
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/timber_edit_bridge.inc
@@ -0,0 +1,429 @@
+/* H-116-TIMBER/1: bounded host adapter over the existing W timber definitions.
+ * Recipes and pending metadata are process-local, never a World or saved file.
+ * No kernel/source/support rule is implemented here. */
+#define TIMBER_EDIT_MAX WORLD_MAX_TIMBERS
+#define TIMBER_RECIPE_MAX 64
+typedef struct {
+    bool sourced, has_override;
+    Coord direct;
+    EngDimensionSource source, override_value;
+} TimberEditDimension;
+typedef struct {
+    TimberEditDimension dimensions[2];
+    WoodMemberRole role;
+    Coord priority;
+} TimberEditDefinition;
+typedef struct {
+    bool used;
+    char key[65];
+    uint64_t revision;
+    uint32_t boundary;
+    TimberEditDefinition definition;
+} TimberEditRecipe;
+typedef struct {
+    uint32_t id;
+    bool dimension_set[2], role_set, priority_set, direction_set;
+    TimberEditDimension dimensions[2];
+    WoodMemberRole role;
+    Coord priority;
+    int direction;
+} TimberEditPatch;
+typedef struct {
+    uint32_t boundary, ids[TIMBER_EDIT_MAX], created_id;
+    size_t count;
+    bool creating;
+} TimberEditPending;
+typedef struct {
+    TimberEditPending pending;
+    TimberEditPatch patches[TIMBER_EDIT_MAX];
+    TimberEditDefinition recipe;
+    char placement[4096];
+    const World *before;
+} TimberEditCall;
+static TimberEditRecipe g_timber_recipes[TIMBER_RECIPE_MAX];
+static size_t g_timber_recipe_next;
+static char g_timber_context_key[65];
+static TimberEditPending g_timber_pending;
+
+static bool timber_edit_random_key(char out[65])
+{
+    unsigned char bytes[32];
+#ifdef _WIN32
+    for(size_t i=0;i<sizeof bytes;i+=sizeof(unsigned int)){
+        unsigned int value;if(rand_s(&value))return false;
+        memcpy(bytes+i,&value,sizeof value);
+    }
+#else
+    FILE *random=fopen("/dev/urandom","rb");if(!random)return false;
+    size_t n=fread(bytes,1,sizeof bytes,random);bool ok=n==sizeof bytes&&!ferror(random);
+    fclose(random);if(!ok)return false;
+#endif
+    for(size_t i=0;i<sizeof bytes;++i)snprintf(out+2*i,3,"%02x",bytes[i]);
+    return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_revision(GeoJson *j,int token,uint64_t *out)
+{
+    char text[21];if(!geo_json_string(j,token,text,sizeof text)||!text[0]||(text[0]=='0'&&text[1]))return false;
+    uint64_t value=0;
+    for(const char *p=text;*p;++p){
+        if(*p<'0'||*p>'9'||value>(UINT64_MAX-(uint64_t)(*p-'0'))/10)return false;
+        value=value*10+(uint64_t)(*p-'0');
+    }
+    *out=value;return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_priority(GeoJson *j,int token,Coord *out)
+{
+    int64_t value;if(geo_json_integer(j,token,&value)){if(value<1)return false;*out=value;return true;}
+    uint64_t exact;if(!timber_edit_revision(j,token,&exact)||!exact||exact>INT64_MAX)return false;
+    *out=(Coord)exact;return true;
+}
+static void timber_edit_json_priority(Coord priority)
+{
+    printf("\"%lld\"",(long long)priority);
+}
+static bool timber_edit_document(const char *path,GeoJson *j,char **text)
+{
+    size_t size;*text=eng_read_file(path,&size);
+    if(!*text){eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_INPUT",g_engineering_error);return false;}
+    if(memchr(*text,0,size)||!utf8_is_valid(*text)){
+        eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_ENCODING","Timber input must be complete UTF-8 without NUL");free(*text);*text=NULL;return false;
+    }
+    if(!app_json_parse_n(j,*text,size)){
+        eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_JSON",j->error);geo_json_free(j);free(*text);*text=NULL;return false;
+    }
+    /* Decoded control characters are not part of any field in this protocol. */
+    for(int i=0;i<j->count;++i)if(j->tokens[i].type==GEO_J_STRING)
+        for(const unsigned char *p=(unsigned char*)j->tokens[i].string;*p;++p)if(*p<32||*p==127){
+            eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_JSON","Timber strings cannot contain control characters");geo_json_free(j);free(*text);*text=NULL;return false;
+        }
+    return true;
+}
+static const char *timber_edit_eligibility(const World *w,uint32_t boundary,uint32_t id)
+{
+    int index=reference_timber_index(w,id);
+    if(index<0)return "The selected timber no longer exists";
+    const Timber *t=&w->wood.timbers[index];const TimberAnchor *a=&w->wood.anchors[index];
+    if(assembly_owned(w,id))return "I42: edit the owning assembly; ordinary timber reuse and batch edits are unavailable";
+    if(!t->placed||!t->auto_length)return "Only ordinary placed D7 timbers are supported";
+    const PlacementSurface *p=world_find_placement(w,a->placement_id);
+    if(!p||p->boundary_id!=boundary)return "Every selected timber must be on the selected face";
+    /* This base World has no #335 group records. Reject unsupported definitions,
+     * rather than accepting a client-supplied ownership/group eligibility flag. */
+    if(t->role<TIMBER_ROLE_BOUNDARY||t->role>TIMBER_ROLE_CUSTOM||a->span_mode!=SPAN_AUTO||
+        (a->anchor_kind!=ANCHOR_AT_LINE&&a->anchor_kind!=ANCHOR_AT_GRID&&a->anchor_kind!=ANCHOR_AT_RULE&&a->anchor_kind!=ANCHOR_AT_EDGE)||
+        a->length_direction<0||a->length_direction>3||
+        (a->depth_side!=SURFACE_DEPTH_POSITIVE_NORMAL&&a->depth_side!=SURFACE_DEPTH_NEGATIVE_NORMAL)||
+        (a->has_through&&a->has_between))return "Unsupported or unknown timber eligibility; the entire selection is blocked";
+    return NULL;
+}
+static void timber_edit_get_definition(const World *w,int index,TimberEditDefinition *out)
+{
+    memset(out,0,sizeof *out);const Timber *t=&w->wood.timbers[index];
+    out->role=t->role;out->priority=t->cut_priority;
+    for(int field=0;field<2;++field){
+        TimberEditDimension *dimension=&out->dimensions[field];EngTarget target={1,t->id,0,field};
+        const EngBinding *binding=eng_binding((World*)w,&target);
+        if(binding){dimension->sourced=true;dimension->source=binding->source;dimension->has_override=binding->has_override;dimension->override_value=binding->override_value;}
+        else dimension->direct=field?t->size_b:t->size_a;
+    }
+}
+static void timber_edit_json_dimension(const TimberEditDimension *dimension)
+{
+    if(!dimension->sourced){printf("{\"mode\":\"direct\",\"value\":%lld}",(long long)dimension->direct);return;}
+    char source[640];eng_source_json(&dimension->source,source,sizeof source);
+    printf("{\"mode\":\"source\",\"source\":%s,\"override\":",source);
+    if(dimension->has_override){eng_source_json(&dimension->override_value,source,sizeof source);fputs(source,stdout);}else fputs("null",stdout);
+    putchar('}');
+}
+static void timber_edit_json_row(const World *w,uint32_t boundary,uint32_t id,bool private_id)
+{
+    int index=reference_timber_index(w,id);if(index<0){fputs("null",stdout);return;}
+    const Timber *t=&w->wood.timbers[index];const TimberAnchor *a=&w->wood.anchors[index];
+    const char *reason=timber_edit_eligibility(w,boundary,id);
+    TimberEditDefinition definition;timber_edit_get_definition(w,index,&definition);
+    if(private_id)fputs("{\"handle\":\"new:0\"",stdout);else printf("{\"id\":%u",id);
+    printf(",\"eligible\":%s,\"reason\":",reason?"false":"true");if(reason)js_string(reason);else fputs("null",stdout);
+    fputs(",\"owner\":",stdout);json_schedule_support_owner(w,id);
+    printf(",\"groups\":[],\"groupsKnown\":true,\"placed\":%s,\"placementOrder\":\"%llu\",\"definition\":{\"A\":",t->placed?"true":"false",(unsigned long long)a->order);
+    timber_edit_json_dimension(&definition.dimensions[0]);fputs(",\"B\":",stdout);timber_edit_json_dimension(&definition.dimensions[1]);
+    fputs(",\"role\":",stdout);js_string(timber_role_name(t->role));printf(",\"priority\":%lld,\"priorityText\":",(long long)t->cut_priority);
+    timber_edit_json_priority(t->cut_priority);printf(",\"placement\":{\"on\":%u,\"anchor\":",a->placement_id);
+    char anchor[1100],selector[2200];reference_timber_placement_text(a,anchor,sizeof anchor,selector,sizeof selector);
+    js_string(anchor);fputs(",\"selector\":",stdout);js_string(selector);fputs(",\"direction\":",stdout);
+    const char *directions[]={"+u","-u","+v","-v"};js_string(a->length_direction>=0&&a->length_direction<4?directions[a->length_direction]:"unsupported");
+    fputs(",\"side\":",stdout);js_string(a->depth_side==SURFACE_DEPTH_POSITIVE_NORMAL?"+n":"-n");
+    printf("}},\"effective\":{\"A\":%lld,\"B\":%lld},\"geometry\":",(long long)t->size_a,(long long)t->size_b);json_reference_geometry(w,index);putchar('}');
+}
+static bool timber_edit_ids(GeoJson *j,int array,uint32_t ids[TIMBER_EDIT_MAX],size_t *count)
+{
+    if(!geo_json_is_array(j,array))return false;int n=geo_json_size(j,array);
+    if(n<1||n>TIMBER_EDIT_MAX)return false;*count=(size_t)n;
+    for(int i=0;i<n;++i){if(!eng_u32(j,geo_json_at(j,array,i),&ids[i],false))return false;
+        for(int k=0;k<i;++k)if(ids[k]==ids[i])return false;}
+    return true;
+}
+static void cmd_timber_edit_read(const char *path)
+{
+    GeoJson j;char *text;if(!timber_edit_document(path,&j,&text))return;
+    char schema[40];uint32_t boundary,ids[TIMBER_EDIT_MAX];size_t count;
+    if(!geo_json_keys(&j,0,"schema,boundaryId,timberIds","")||!geo_json_string(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"schema"),schema,sizeof schema)||
+        strcmp(schema,"timber-edit-read/1")||!eng_u32(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"boundaryId"),&boundary,false)||
+        !world_find_boundary(&g_world,boundary)||!timber_edit_ids(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"timberIds"),ids,&count)){
+        eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_SCHEMA","Expected timber-edit-read/1, an existing boundary and 1..64 unique timber ids");goto done;
+    }
+    const char *reason=NULL;for(size_t i=0;i<count;++i){const char *why=timber_edit_eligibility(&g_world,boundary,ids[i]);if(why&&!reason)reason=why;}
+    if(!g_timber_context_key[0]&&!timber_edit_random_key(g_timber_context_key)){
+        eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_RANDOM","Cannot create an opaque native session key");goto done;
+    }
+    TimberEditRecipe *recipe=NULL;
+    if(count==1&&!reason){
+        recipe=&g_timber_recipes[g_timber_recipe_next%TIMBER_RECIPE_MAX];
+        TimberEditRecipe next={0};if(!timber_edit_random_key(next.key)){eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_RANDOM","Cannot create an opaque recipe key");goto done;}
+        next.used=true;next.revision=g_definition_revision;next.boundary=boundary;
+        timber_edit_get_definition(&g_world,reference_timber_index(&g_world,ids[0]),&next.definition);*recipe=next;++g_timber_recipe_next;
+    }
+    printf("{\"ok\":true,\"schema\":\"timber-edit-read-result/1\",\"revision\":\"%llu\",\"contextKey\":\"%s\",\"boundaryId\":%u,\"maxOperations\":%d,\"eligible\":%s,\"reason\":",(unsigned long long)g_definition_revision,g_timber_context_key,boundary,TIMBER_EDIT_MAX,reason?"false":"true");
+    if(reason)js_string(reason);else fputs("null",stdout);fputs(",\"timbers\":[",stdout);
+    for(size_t i=0;i<count;++i){if(i)putchar(',');timber_edit_json_row(&g_world,boundary,ids[i],false);}
+    fputs("],\"recipeKey\":",stdout);if(recipe)js_string(recipe->key);else fputs("null",stdout);fputs("}\n",stdout);
+done:geo_json_free(&j);free(text);
+}
+static bool timber_edit_parse_dimension(GeoJson *j,int token,TimberEditDimension *out)
+{
+    memset(out,0,sizeof *out);char mode[16];
+    if(!geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,token,"mode"),mode,sizeof mode))return eng_fail("A/B require an explicit direct or source mode");
+    if(!strcmp(mode,"direct")){
+        if(!geo_json_keys(j,token,"mode,value","")||!eng_coord(j,geo_json_member(j,token,"value"),&out->direct,true))return eng_fail("Direct A/B requires one positive integer value");
+        return true;
+    }
+    if(strcmp(mode,"source")||!geo_json_keys(j,token,"mode,source,override","")||
+        !eng_source_parse(j,geo_json_member(j,token,"source"),&out->source,true))return eng_fail("Source A/B requires its complete typed source and explicit override");
+    int override=geo_json_member(j,token,"override");out->sourced=true;out->has_override=!geo_json_is_null(j,override);
+    if(out->has_override&&!eng_source_parse(j,override,&out->override_value,false))return false;
+    return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_parse_patch(GeoJson *j,int operation,TimberEditPatch *patch)
+{
+    if(!geo_json_keys(j,operation,"op,id,fields","")||!eng_u32(j,geo_json_member(j,operation,"id"),&patch->id,false))return eng_fail("update-timber-fields needs an id and fields");
+    int fields=geo_json_member(j,operation,"fields");
+    if(!geo_json_keys(j,fields,"","A,B,role,priority,direction"))return eng_fail("Unknown batch field; only A/B, role, priority and direction are supported");
+    for(int field=0;field<2;++field){int token=geo_json_member(j,fields,field?"B":"A");patch->dimension_set[field]=token>=0;
+        if(token>=0&&!timber_edit_parse_dimension(j,token,&patch->dimensions[field]))return false;}
+    char value[24];int token=geo_json_member(j,fields,"role");patch->role_set=token>=0;
+    if(token>=0){if(!geo_json_string(j,token,value,sizeof value))return eng_fail("Invalid timber role");
+        if(!strcmp(value,"boundary"))patch->role=TIMBER_ROLE_BOUNDARY;else if(!strcmp(value,"primary"))patch->role=TIMBER_ROLE_PRIMARY;
+        else if(!strcmp(value,"secondary"))patch->role=TIMBER_ROLE_SECONDARY;else if(!strcmp(value,"custom"))patch->role=TIMBER_ROLE_CUSTOM;else return eng_fail("Unknown timber role");}
+    token=geo_json_member(j,fields,"priority");patch->priority_set=token>=0;
+    if(token>=0&&!timber_edit_priority(j,token,&patch->priority))return eng_fail("Priority requires a canonical positive int64 integer or string");
+    token=geo_json_member(j,fields,"direction");patch->direction_set=token>=0;
+    if(token>=0&&(!geo_json_string(j,token,value,sizeof value)||!lookup_keyword(value,LENGTH_DIRECTION_WORDS,&patch->direction)))return eng_fail("Direction must be +u, -u, +v or -v");
+    return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_set_dimension(World *w,int index,int field,const TimberEditDimension *dimension)
+{
+    EngTarget target={1,w->wood.timbers[index].id,0,field};
+    if(dimension->sourced){
+        if(!eng_bind(w,&target,&dimension->source))return false;
+        EngBinding *binding=eng_binding(w,&target);if(!binding)return eng_fail("Cannot retain dimension source");
+        binding->has_override=dimension->has_override;binding->override_value=dimension->override_value;
+    }else{
+        /* Removing this one binding is explicit; every omitted field stays intact. */
+        for(size_t i=0;i<w->wood.engineering.binding_count;++i)if(eng_same_target(&w->wood.engineering.bindings[i].target,&target)){
+            size_t left=w->wood.engineering.binding_count-i-1;
+            memmove(&w->wood.engineering.bindings[i],&w->wood.engineering.bindings[i+1],left*sizeof(EngBinding));
+            --w->wood.engineering.binding_count;break;
+        }
+        if(field)w->wood.timbers[index].size_b=dimension->direct;else w->wood.timbers[index].size_a=dimension->direct;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_apply_patch(World *w,const TimberEditPatch *patch)
+{
+    int index=reference_timber_index(w,patch->id);if(index<0)return eng_fail("Selected timber disappeared");
+    Timber *timber=&w->wood.timbers[index];
+    WoodMemberRole role=patch->role_set?patch->role:timber->role;Coord priority=timber->cut_priority;
+    if(role==TIMBER_ROLE_CUSTOM){
+        if(patch->role_set&&!patch->priority_set)return eng_fail("An explicitly selected custom role also requires its explicit priority");
+        if(patch->priority_set)priority=patch->priority;
+    }else{
+        if(patch->priority_set)return eng_fail("Only custom timbers can declare priority");
+        priority=(Coord)role+1;
+    }
+    timber->role=role;timber->cut_priority=priority;
+    for(int field=0;field<2;++field)if(patch->dimension_set[field]&&!timber_edit_set_dimension(w,index,field,&patch->dimensions[field]))return false;
+    if(patch->direction_set)w->wood.anchors[index].length_direction=patch->direction;
+    return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_parse_create(GeoJson *j,int operation,TimberEditCall *call)
+{
+    char key[65],anchor[1100],selector[2200],direction[8],side[8];uint32_t on;
+    if(!geo_json_keys(j,operation,"op,recipeKey,placement","")||!geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,operation,"recipeKey"),key,sizeof key))return eng_fail("create-from-recipe needs an opaque recipeKey and placement");
+    TimberEditRecipe *recipe=NULL;
+    for(size_t i=0;i<TIMBER_RECIPE_MAX;++i)if(g_timber_recipes[i].used&&!strcmp(g_timber_recipes[i].key,key)){recipe=&g_timber_recipes[i];break;}
+    if(!recipe||recipe->revision!=g_definition_revision||recipe->boundary!=call->pending.boundary)return eng_fail("Recipe expired or belongs to another native context, revision or face");
+    int placement=geo_json_member(j,operation,"placement");
+    if(!geo_json_keys(j,placement,"on,anchor,selector,direction,side","")||!eng_u32(j,geo_json_member(j,placement,"on"),&on,false)||
+        !geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,placement,"anchor"),anchor,sizeof anchor)||
+        !geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,placement,"selector"),selector,sizeof selector)||
+        !geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,placement,"direction"),direction,sizeof direction)||
+        !geo_json_string(j,geo_json_member(j,placement,"side"),side,sizeof side))return eng_fail("Placement requires an explicit on, anchor, selector, direction and side");
+    const PlacementSurface *surface=world_find_placement(&g_world,on);int parsed;
+    if(!surface||surface->boundary_id!=call->pending.boundary)return eng_fail("Paste/Repeat must select a placement on the same face");
+    if(!lookup_keyword(direction,LENGTH_DIRECTION_WORDS,&parsed)||!lookup_keyword(side,DEPTH_SIDE_WORDS,&parsed))return eng_fail("Invalid placement direction or side");
+    if(strncmp(anchor,"at-line ",8)&&strncmp(anchor,"at-face ",8))return eng_fail("Placement needs one complete at-line or at-face anchor");
+    if(selector[0]&&strncmp(selector,"through ",8)&&strncmp(selector,"between ",8))return eng_fail("Placement selector must be empty, through or between");
+    char anchor_copy[sizeof anchor],selector_copy[sizeof selector];char *parts[16];
+    snprintf(anchor_copy,sizeof anchor_copy,"%s",anchor);int n=split_tokens(anchor_copy,parts,16);
+    bool clause=n==2&&!strcmp(parts[0],"at-face");
+    if(n>=3&&!strcmp(parts[0],"at-line"))clause=
+        (!strcmp(parts[1],"guide")&&n==3)||(!strcmp(parts[1],"grid")&&(n==5||(n==7&&!strcmp(parts[5],"band"))))||(!strcmp(parts[1],"rule")&&n==9);
+    if(!clause)return eng_fail("Anchor must contain exactly one complete anchor clause");
+    snprintf(selector_copy,sizeof selector_copy,"%s",selector);n=split_tokens(selector_copy,parts,16);
+    if(n&&!(n==2&&!strcmp(parts[0],"through"))&&!(n==3&&!strcmp(parts[0],"between")))return eng_fail("Selector must contain exactly one complete selector clause");
+    if(g_world.wood.timber_count>=WORLD_MAX_TIMBERS)return eng_fail("Timber capacity is full");
+    /* The number exists only in the isolated candidate. No live ID is reserved,
+     * and preview exposes new:0 instead; publication is the allocation point. */
+    WORLD_NEXT_ID(call->pending.created_id,g_world.wood.timbers,g_world.wood.timber_count,false);
+    int written=snprintf(call->placement,sizeof call->placement,"place %u on %u %s%s%s %s %s",call->pending.created_id,on,anchor,selector[0]?" ":"",selector,direction,side);
+    if(written<0||(size_t)written>=sizeof call->placement)return eng_fail("Placement is too long");
+    call->recipe=recipe->definition;return true;
+}
+static bool timber_edit_evaluate(void *after,GeoEvalContext *sources,void *opaque)
+{
+    (void)sources;World *candidate=after;TimberEditCall *call=opaque;bool ok=true;
+    const World *previous=g_subgrid_before;bool capturing=g_reference_capturing;
+    g_subgrid_before=call->before;g_reference_capturing=true;
+    if(call->pending.creating){
+        size_t index=candidate->wood.timber_count++;
+        ok=wood_member_construct(call->pending.created_id,0,1,1,true,call->recipe.role,call->recipe.priority,&candidate->wood.timbers[index]);
+        memset(&candidate->wood.anchors[index],0,sizeof candidate->wood.anchors[index]);
+        for(int field=0;field<2&&ok;++field)ok=timber_edit_set_dimension(candidate,(int)index,field,&call->recipe.dimensions[field]);
+        /* One create operation uses the existing complete placement parser and
+         * its common final validation; no derived placement is reconstructed. */
+        char command[sizeof call->placement];snprintf(command,sizeof command,"%s",call->placement);
+        char *tokens[64];int count=split_tokens(command,tokens,64);
+        g_reference_trial=*candidate;
+        if(ok)ok=apply_reference_place("timber-edit",1,tokens,count,candidate);
+    }else{
+        /* I8: all explicit definitions first; no per-member validation/commit. */
+        for(size_t i=0;i<call->pending.count&&ok;++i)ok=timber_edit_apply_patch(candidate,&call->patches[i]);
+        g_reference_trial=*candidate;
+        if(ok)ok=world_revalidate(candidate);
+    }
+    for(size_t i=0;i<candidate->wood.timber_count&&ok;++i)if(!candidate->wood.timbers[i].placed)ok=eng_fail("I9: candidate contains an unplaced timber");
+    if(!ok){*candidate=g_reference_trial;if(!g_engineering_error[0])eng_fail(g_revalidate_error[0]?g_revalidate_error:scene_last_error()[0]?scene_last_error():"I8: timber candidate rejected");}
+    g_subgrid_before=previous;g_reference_capturing=capturing;
+    return ok;
+}
+static void timber_edit_json_message(const char *message,const TimberEditPending *pending)
+{
+    if(!pending->creating){js_string(message);return;}
+    /* Native diagnostic strings also name timbers. Map the candidate-local
+     * number to its private handle without changing dimensions or clause ids. */
+    const char *prefixes[]={"timber ","timber:","角材 ","角材（","角材("};
+    char out[4096];size_t used=0;const char *p=message;
+    while(*p&&used+1<sizeof out){bool replaced=false;
+        for(size_t i=0;i<sizeof prefixes/sizeof prefixes[0];++i){
+            char needle[80];int n=snprintf(needle,sizeof needle,"%s%u",prefixes[i],pending->created_id);
+            if(n>0&&!strncmp(p,needle,(size_t)n)&&(p[n]<'0'||p[n]>'9')){
+                int wrote=snprintf(out+used,sizeof out-used,"%snew:0",prefixes[i]);
+                if(wrote<0||(size_t)wrote>=sizeof out-used){out[used]=0;js_string(out);return;}
+                used+=(size_t)wrote;p+=n;replaced=true;break;
+            }
+        }
+        if(!replaced)out[used++]=*p++;
+    }
+    out[used]=0;js_string(out);
+}
+static void timber_edit_json_preview(const AppWorldCandidate *transaction,const TimberEditPending *pending,bool valid,bool no_op,const char *error)
+{
+    printf("{\"schema\":\"timber-edit-preview-result/1\",\"token\":");if(no_op)fputs("null",stdout);else printf("%u",g_preview.token);
+    printf(",\"valid\":%s,\"revision\":\"%llu\",\"noOp\":%s,\"before\":[",valid?"true":"false",(unsigned long long)g_definition_revision,no_op?"true":"false");
+    if(!pending->creating)for(size_t i=0;i<pending->count;++i){if(i)putchar(',');timber_edit_json_row(&transaction->before,pending->boundary,pending->ids[i],false);}
+    fputs("],\"after\":[",stdout);
+    if(pending->creating)timber_edit_json_row(&transaction->after,pending->boundary,pending->created_id,true);
+    else for(size_t i=0;i<pending->count;++i){if(i)putchar(',');timber_edit_json_row(&transaction->after,pending->boundary,pending->ids[i],false);}
+    fputs("],\"changes\":[",stdout);
+    if(!no_op){if(pending->creating)fputs("{\"op\":\"create-from-recipe\",\"handle\":\"new:0\"}",stdout);
+        else for(size_t i=0;i<pending->count;++i)printf("%s{\"op\":\"update-timber-fields\",\"id\":%u}",i?",":"",pending->ids[i]);}
+    fputs("],\"dependencies\":{\"timbers\":",stdout);json_candidate_timbers(&transaction->before,&transaction->after,false);
+    fputs(",\"guides\":",stdout);json_candidate_guides(&transaction->before,&transaction->after);fputs("},\"violations\":[",stdout);
+    if(!valid){fputs("{\"code\":\"TIMBER_INVALID\",\"clause\":\"I8/I8a\",\"message\":",stdout);timber_edit_json_message(error,pending);putchar('}');}
+    if(!no_op)for(size_t i=0;i<g_preview.issue_count;++i){const ReferenceIssue *issue=&g_preview.issues[i];printf("%s{",!valid||i?",":"");
+        if(pending->creating&&issue->timber==pending->created_id)fputs("\"handle\":\"new:0\"",stdout);else printf("\"timber\":%u",issue->timber);
+        fputs(",\"clause\":",stdout);js_string(issue->clause);fputs(",\"message\":",stdout);timber_edit_json_message(issue->actual,pending);putchar('}');}
+    fputs("]}",stdout);
+}
+static void cmd_timber_edit_preview(const char *path)
+{
+    if(g_history.grouping){eng_bridge_error("HISTORY_GROUP","Finish the existing history group first");return;}
+    GeoJson j;char *text;if(!timber_edit_document(path,&j,&text))return;
+    TimberEditCall call={0};char schema[40];uint64_t revision;int operations=geo_json_member(&j,0,"operations");
+    if(!geo_json_keys(&j,0,"schema,expectedRevision,boundaryId,operations","")||!geo_json_string(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"schema"),schema,sizeof schema)||strcmp(schema,"timber-edit-transaction/1")||
+        !timber_edit_revision(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"expectedRevision"),&revision)||!eng_u32(&j,geo_json_member(&j,0,"boundaryId"),&call.pending.boundary,false)||
+        !world_find_boundary(&g_world,call.pending.boundary)||!geo_json_is_array(&j,operations)){
+        eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_SCHEMA","Expected timber-edit-transaction/1 with canonical uint64 revision string, face and operations");goto done;
+    }
+    if(revision!=g_definition_revision){eng_bridge_error("STALE","Timber definitions changed; read and preview again");goto done;}
+    int count=geo_json_size(&j,operations);if(count<1||count>TIMBER_EDIT_MAX){eng_bridge_error("CAPACITY","Timber batch needs 1..64 operations");goto done;}
+    call.pending.count=(size_t)count;g_engineering_error[0]=0;
+    for(int i=0;i<count;++i){int operation=geo_json_at(&j,operations,i);char op[32];
+        if(!geo_json_string(&j,geo_json_member(&j,operation,"op"),op,sizeof op)){eng_fail("Every operation needs a supported op");goto invalid;}
+        if(!strcmp(op,"create-from-recipe")){
+            if(count!=1){eng_fail("A reuse transaction creates exactly one timber and cannot mix updates");goto invalid;}
+            call.pending.creating=true;if(!timber_edit_parse_create(&j,operation,&call))goto invalid;
+        }else if(!strcmp(op,"update-timber-fields")){
+            if(!timber_edit_parse_patch(&j,operation,&call.patches[i]))goto invalid;
+            call.pending.ids[i]=call.patches[i].id;
+            for(int k=0;k<i;++k)if(call.pending.ids[k]==call.pending.ids[i]){eng_fail("Duplicate timber target");goto invalid;}
+            const char *reason=timber_edit_eligibility(&g_world,call.pending.boundary,call.pending.ids[i]);if(reason){eng_fail(reason);goto invalid;}
+        }else{eng_fail("Unsupported timber edit operation");goto invalid;}
+    }
+    {
+        AppWorldCandidate *transaction=app_world_candidate_create(&g_world);if(!transaction){eng_bridge_error("MEMORY","Cannot allocate timber candidate");goto done;}
+        call.before=&transaction->before;g_reference_issue_count=0;g_scene_last_error[0]=0;g_revalidate_error[0]=0;
+        bool valid=app_world_candidate_evaluate(transaction,timber_edit_evaluate,&call)==GEO_CANDIDATE_OK;
+        if(valid&&!app_world_candidate_seal(transaction)){valid=false;eng_fail("I8: cannot seal timber candidate");}
+        char error[512];snprintf(error,sizeof error,"%s",g_engineering_error[0]?g_engineering_error:"Candidate rejected");
+        bool no_op=valid&&world_definitions_equal(&transaction->before,&transaction->after);
+        if(!no_op){char *label="timber-edit-transaction";reference_stage(transaction,&label,1,valid);
+            g_preview.engineering_batch=true;g_preview.timber_batch=true;g_timber_pending=call.pending;}
+        fputs("{\"ok\":true,\"preview\":",stdout);timber_edit_json_preview(transaction,&call.pending,valid,no_op,error);fputs("}\n",stdout);
+        if(no_op)app_world_candidate_destroy(transaction);
+    }
+    goto done;
+invalid:eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_INPUT",g_engineering_error[0]?g_engineering_error:"Invalid timber request");
+done:geo_json_free(&j);free(text);
+}
+static void cmd_timber_edit_confirm(const char *arg)
+{
+    uint32_t token;
+    if(!arg||!parse_u32(arg,&token)||!g_preview.ready||!g_preview.timber_batch||g_preview.token!=token||!g_preview.valid){eng_bridge_error("TOKEN","Missing, invalid or unrelated timber preview token");return;}
+    if(g_history.grouping||g_preview.revision!=g_definition_revision||!app_world_candidate_current(g_preview.transaction,&g_world)){
+        g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("STALE","Timber preview expired; read and preview again");return;
+    }
+    if(!g_timber_pending.creating)for(size_t i=0;i<g_timber_pending.count;++i){const char *reason=timber_edit_eligibility(&g_world,g_timber_pending.boundary,g_timber_pending.ids[i]);
+        if(reason){g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("TIMBER_ELIGIBILITY",reason);return;}}
+    if(!app_world_candidate_revalidate(g_preview.transaction,&g_world)){
+        g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("INVALID",g_revalidate_error);return;
+    }
+    if(world_definitions_equal(&g_world,&g_preview.transaction->after)){
+        g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("NO_OP","No-op timber edits have no commit token");return;
+    }
+    if(g_definition_revision==UINT64_MAX){g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("CAPACITY","Definition revision capacity is exhausted; World unchanged");return;}
+    uint64_t before=g_definition_revision;history_record(&g_world);
+    if(!app_world_candidate_publish(g_preview.transaction,&g_world,true)){
+        history_unrecord();g_preview.ready=false;eng_bridge_error("STALE","Timber candidate changed before publication");return;
+    }
+    ++g_definition_revision;g_preview.ready=false;
+    printf("{\"ok\":true,\"history_changed\":true,\"revision\":\"%llu\",\"before_revision\":\"%llu\",\"transaction_id\":%u,\"changedIds\":[",(unsigned long long)g_definition_revision,(unsigned long long)before,token);
+    if(!g_timber_pending.creating)for(size_t i=0;i<g_timber_pending.count;++i)printf("%s%u",i?",":"",g_timber_pending.ids[i]);
+    fputs("],\"created\":[",stdout);if(g_timber_pending.creating)printf("{\"handle\":\"new:0\",\"kind\":\"timber\",\"id\":%u}",g_timber_pending.created_id);fputs("]}\n",stdout);
+}
+static void cmd_timber_edit_cancel(const char *arg)
+{
+    uint32_t token;if(!arg||!parse_u32(arg,&token)){eng_bridge_error("TOKEN","Cancel requires a timber preview token");return;}
+    bool cancelled=g_preview.ready&&g_preview.timber_batch&&g_preview.token==token;
+    if(cancelled){g_preview.ready=false;app_candidate_discard(g_preview.transaction->control);}
+    printf("{\"ok\":true,\"cancelled\":%s,\"revision\":\"%llu\"}\n",cancelled?"true":"false",(unsigned long long)g_definition_revision);
+}
diff --git a/geometry-web/timber_edit_transport.py b/geometry-web/timber_edit_transport.py
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/timber_edit_transport.py
@@ -0,0 +1,104 @@
+"""H-116-TIMBER/1 bounded transport; native owns recipes and all geometry."""
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import tempfile
+
+MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
+MAX_OPERATIONS = 64
+
+
+def _error(message):
+    return json.dumps({'ok': False, 'code': 'TIMBER_INPUT', 'error': message},
+                      ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')
+
+
+def _pairs(pairs):
+    result = {}
+    for key, value in pairs:
+        if key in result:
+            raise ValueError('JSON 不得包含重複欄位')
+        result[key] = value
+    return result
+
+
+def _constant(_value):
+    raise ValueError('JSON 不接受非有限數值')
+
+
+def _strings(value):
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if any(ord(char) < 32 or ord(char) == 127 for char in value):
+            raise ValueError('角材資料字串不得含控制字元')
+    elif isinstance(value, list):
+        for item in value:
+            _strings(item)
+    elif isinstance(value, dict):
+        for key, item in value.items():
+            _strings(key)
+            _strings(item)
+
+
+def _uint32(value):
+    return type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 4294967295
+
+
+def file_request(body, ask_bridge, *, read=False):
+    """The service creates/deletes the file; caller-controlled paths are rejected."""
+    if not isinstance(body, bytes) or not 0 < len(body) <= MAX_BYTES:
+        return _error('角材資料必須是完整位元組，且不得超過 1 MiB')
+    try:
+        text = body.decode('utf-8')
+        data = json.loads(text, object_pairs_hook=_pairs, parse_constant=_constant)
+        _strings(data)
+        keys = {'schema', 'boundaryId', 'timberIds'} if read else {
+            'schema', 'boundaryId', 'expectedRevision', 'operations'}
+        if (type(data) is not dict or set(data) != keys or
+                not _uint32(data.get('boundaryId')) or
+                data.get('schema') != ('timber-edit-read/1' if read else 'timber-edit-transaction/1')):
+            raise ValueError('角材資料需要正確版本、面識別及明確欄位')
+        if read:
+            ids = data['timberIds']
+            if (type(ids) is not list or not 1 <= len(ids) <= MAX_OPERATIONS or
+                    not all(_uint32(item) for item in ids) or len(set(ids)) != len(ids)):
+                raise ValueError('讀取需要 1–64 個不重複的角材識別')
+        else:
+            revision = data['expectedRevision']
+            if (type(revision) is not str or not re.fullmatch(r'0|[1-9][0-9]{0,19}', revision) or
+                    int(revision) > 18446744073709551615):
+                raise ValueError('來源版本必須是完整 uint64 十進位字串')
+            operations = data['operations']
+            if type(operations) is not list or not 1 <= len(operations) <= MAX_OPERATIONS:
+                raise ValueError('交易需要 1–64 個操作')
+            targets = set()
+            for operation in operations:
+                if type(operation) is not dict:
+                    raise ValueError('角材操作必須是物件')
+                if operation.get('op') == 'update-timber-fields':
+                    if (set(operation) != {'op', 'id', 'fields'} or not _uint32(operation.get('id')) or
+                            operation['id'] in targets or type(operation.get('fields')) is not dict or
+                            set(operation['fields']) - {'A', 'B', 'role', 'priority', 'direction'}):
+                        raise ValueError('批改只接受不重複角材的明選欄位')
+                    targets.add(operation['id'])
+                elif operation.get('op') == 'create-from-recipe':
+                    if (len(operations) != 1 or set(operation) != {'op', 'recipeKey', 'placement'} or
+                            type(operation.get('recipeKey')) is not str or
+                            not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{64}', operation['recipeKey']) or
+                            type(operation.get('placement')) is not dict or
+                            set(operation['placement']) != {'on', 'anchor', 'selector', 'direction', 'side'} or
+                            not _uint32(operation['placement'].get('on'))):
+                        raise ValueError('貼上／重複需要一個原生配方及完整明選落點')
+                else:
+                    raise ValueError('不支援的角材操作')
+    except (ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError) as error:
+        return _error(str(error))
+    path = None
+    try:
+        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile('wb', suffix='.json', delete=False) as stream:
+            stream.write(body)
+            path = Path(stream.name)
+        command = 'timber-edit-read' if read else 'timber-edit-preview'
+        return ask_bridge(f'{command} {path}')
+    finally:
+        if path is not None:
+            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
diff --git a/geometry-web/docs/H-116-TIMBER-1.md b/geometry-web/docs/H-116-TIMBER-1.md
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/docs/H-116-TIMBER-1.md
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+# H-116-TIMBER/1 — R11 第二片 A 的原生操作契約
+
+依據 issue #116 決策 6436163515、設計 6436046251 第 4–6 節與第 8 節；
+基底為 `5c5700b5c151390f8f5c22a74655ee446ecfd2f0`。本 adapter 屬 H，
+呼叫既有 K candidate/source 與 W 角材、工程來源、參照放置、支承及全域驗證。
+它不新增 K/W 幾何規則，也不增加儲存檔能力或持久配方格式。
+
+## 範圍與守門
+
+同一原生專案 context、同一 boundary 面，單支普通已放置 D7 的複製／貼上／重複，
+以及最多 64 支同面普通已放置角材的欄位批改。合法 timber id 與 placement id 包含 0。
+更新只准明選 A、B、role、priority、direction；省略的定義保持各自原值。
+批改不允許修改錨點、側向、存在身份或放置順序。
+
+原生從目前 World 檢查存在、普通 D7、已放置、同面、assembly ownership 與已支援
+placement discriminator；任一不適用，整筆拒絕。客戶端不能用 eligible、owner 或
+groups 旗標覆寫判定。這個固定基底沒有 #335 群組資料模型，因此 `groups:[]`、
+`groupsKnown:true` 表示原生已知本版 World schema 無此類群組記錄，不表示支援群組
+複製。未知請求欄位、未支援物件及未知定義必須拒絕；加入群組模型時須升級此守門，
+不能把 group 當 owner，也不能據本契約允許任意子圖、跨面、跨專案。
+
+基底未提供 D60 incarnation ledger；本片保留既有 timber/anchor record slot、id、
+放置 order，使用完整 revision 與同一 sealed candidate 守護，不宣稱新增 incarnation。
+
+## 傳輸與容量
+
+`POST /timber-edit/read` 與 `POST /timber-edit/preview` 接完整 UTF-8 JSON，
+最多 1 MiB。拒重複 key、未知欄位、NUL、字串控制字元、重複目標及超額操作。
+Python transport 建立服務擁有的暫存檔，送 `timber-edit-read <path>` 或
+`timber-edit-preview <path>`，在 finally 移除；外部 JSON 不接受檔案路徑。
+原生再次做完整 schema 與語意檢查，Python 不計算來源值或幾何。
+
+`expectedRevision` 與回覆 revision 都是 canonical uint64 十進位字串，
+不是 JavaScript Number。0 與 18446744073709551615 都可解析；數字型、正負號、
+前導零、溢位皆拒絕。若原生 revision 已達上限，拒絕會遞增的提交，不回繞。
+
+## 唯讀取得
+
+```json
+{"schema":"timber-edit-read/1","boundaryId":1,"timberIds":[0]}
+```
+
+回覆 `schema:"timber-edit-read-result/1"`、`revision`、`contextKey`、
+`boundaryId`、`maxOperations:64`、`eligible`、`reason`、`timbers`、`recipeKey`。
+`contextKey` 是每個 bridge 啟動時產生的隨機 64 hex 識別；只有一支適用角材才取得
+獨立隨機 recipeKey。配方最多保留 64 份；最舊可被逐出，失效必須重新讀取。
+亂數失敗直接拒絕，不退回時間或可預測計數器。
+
+每個 row 含 id、eligible/reason、typed owner、groups/groupsKnown、placed、
+以字串表示的 placementOrder、definition、effective 與唯讀 geometry。
+definition.A/B 各為下列一種，兩者不是等價定義：
+
+```json
+{"mode":"direct","value":27}
+```
+
+```json
+{"mode":"source","source":{"kind":"parameter","parameterId":10,"fieldId":5},"override":null}
+```
+
+source 沿用既有 literal/spec/material/application/parameter 型別，完整保存 material
+id/version/field 或 parameterId/fieldId；override 必須明示 null 或既有合法來源。
+effective.A/B 只供顯示，不可用來反推 source。definition 另含 role、priority 與
+placement `{on,anchor,selector,direction,side}`；錨點文字直接序列化已存參照，
+不從幾何重建。衍生長度、頂點與軸向不屬於可重用定義。
+
+`definition.priorityText` 永遠提供 canonical 正 int64 十進位字串，是 UI 顯示、比較
+與編輯優先級的權威值。既有 `priority` JSON 整數保留作相容投影，瀏覽器不得拿它
+還原大整數。fields.priority 接受既有正 int64 JSON 整數或 canonical 正 int64 字串；
+UI 一律送字串，範圍 1..9223372036854775807。priority 是 D15 的比較序，不能套用
+尺寸的座標上限。舊資料若缺 priorityText，只能接受安全正整數；超出安全範圍須守門，
+不可悄悄四捨五入。opaque 原生 recipe 保留完整 int64，與顯示投影無關。
+
+讀取不改 World、history、dirty、revision 或既有 pending。配方只保留 A/B 的
+typed 定義與 override、role/priority，以及 context/revision/面綁定；不存原物件 id、
+owner、incarnation、長度或幾何。Undo/Redo 即使回到相同 dump，revision 仍不同，
+舊 recipe 必須失效；另一次 process 啟動也不接受舊 key。
+
+## 預覽
+
+```json
+{"schema":"timber-edit-transaction/1","expectedRevision":"15","boundaryId":1,"operations":[{"op":"update-timber-fields","id":0,"fields":{"A":{"mode":"direct","value":29}}}]}
+```
+
+create transaction 只容許一個 `create-from-recipe`，不得混入更新：
+
+```json
+{"op":"create-from-recipe","recipeKey":"<原生 64 hex key>","placement":{"on":0,"anchor":"at-face e2","selector":"","direction":"+u","side":"+n"}}
+```
+
+使用者必須明選合法落點；不複製原位置、不自動偏移。anchor 恰為一條既有 at-line
+或 at-face 子句，selector 為空、through 或 between 子句。拒絕控制字元、尾隨指令
+與跨面 placement。使用既有完整 placement parser 與幾何驗證。
+
+批改先解析所有 patch、檢查所有目標，在同一 AppWorldCandidate 中依次寫入全部
+明選定義，最後才呼叫一次 world_revalidate；不走逐支 delete/recreate 或逐支提交。
+明選 direct 只移除該欄位自己的工程綁定；其他欄位來源不動。明選 custom role
+須同時提供正整數 priority；離開 custom 恢復該普通 role 的既有優先級。
+
+回覆 `ok:true, preview`，包含 schema、token、valid、revision、noOp、before/after、
+changes、dependencies.timbers/guides 及 violations。有效且完整定義相同時
+`noOp:true,token:null`，不建立新 pending、不提交、不增 history/revision。
+相同有效值但不同 source identity 是真正變更。
+
+新物件預覽使用 `handle:"new:0"`。候選內只用私有數值索引進行原生計算；正式 World
+與配號不變，after/changes/diagnostics 不對外露出此數值作為新正式 timber id。
+既有依賴維持自己的正式 typed id。真正成功發布時才回傳新增正式 id 映射。
+uint32 配號回繞必須跳過所有已存在 id，包括 0。
+
+語法/適用性/stale 錯誤回 ok:false，完全不建立新 pending；幾何錯誤回
+ok:true、valid:false 及自己的 token，保留完整候選違規供檢閱與取消。
+共用 preview slot 仍由現有候選系統持有，前端必須持有全域 workspace lease，
+不讓其他寫入/預覽穿越不確定結果；read/no-op 不取消他人的 pending。
+
+## 確認、取消與 requestId
+
+`timber-edit-confirm <token>` 只接受 timber family 的當前有效 token，重新檢查
+revision、target eligibility、candidate seal，重驗原候選後記一次 history、發布一次、
+revision 加一。失敗原 World 不動。成功回 history_changed、revision、before_revision、
+transaction_id、changedIds，以及 created 的 `{handle,kind:"timber",id}` 映射。
+再次確認同 token 不會重做。成功後若要 Repeat，先讀取成功新增 id 取得新 baseline 的
+recipeKey；不得把一次批改當作上次新增動作。
+
+`timber-edit-cancel <token>` 只取消自己同 token 的 pending。engineering、assembly、
+section-profile、panel-fill 與一般 line confirm 都不能借用 timber token；反向亦同。
+一般無 token 的 line cancel-preview 不能取消 timber pending。
+
+確認使用既有 `/api` requestId/receipt transport。app.js 必須把 timber-edit-confirm
+列入既有寫入與 requestId 流程；ReferenceWorkspace 持有確認 lease，ContextHistory
+觀察同一成功提交。掉回覆時只用原 requestId 查 receipt，不能重送確認或以畫面猜成功。
+配方、剪貼簿、未確認候選及 repeat 記錄皆不寫入 .gcanvas。
+
+## 驗證界線
+
+`tests/test_timber_edit.py` 是真 bridge 具名測試，包含私有預覽資料與 uint32 回繞。
+`tests/mutate_timber_edit.py` 在隔離 source copy
+逐案編譯 -O0 ELF，僅把對應具名斷言失敗列為 kill，並還原 bytes、重編、重跑綠。
+編譯失敗、崩潰、無關失敗、error、skip 都不是 kill；收據包含 source/ELF/判準雜湊。
+正常產品另以 -O2 編譯並執行全部具名案例。真 UI/CDP、WSL、本機完整 canvas golden
+應依實際各自收據報告，這份契約本身不代表任何測試已通過。
```

### 2026-10-29T06:43:40Z ZhiQiYang

## 【ChatGPT 實作】R11 第二片 — 5/6（補丁 P：同面角材 UI adapter）

基底：`feat/116-object-canvas-menu-20261027 @ 5c5700b5c151390f8f5c22a74655ee446ecfd2f0`。交付時間：2026-10-29 14:42 Asia/Taipei。

本片：`05-R11-P-ui-adapter.patch`；UTF-8、LF；**37,893 bytes**。

**SHA-256：`06be3797d79180982d148cc3345da1f89400c4bfb77997967698f43868382154`**

改動檔清單：

- `geometry-web/timber-edit.js`

## UI P 的行為

新增 `TimberEdit` adapter，接第一片的同一面內選單與右欄：

- 普通單支「複製」只讀取定義；貼上與重複使用同一定位表單，每次明選新承載與錨點。沒有自動偏移或複製原位置；完成原生建立後才可 Repeat，批改不能成為 Repeat 的建立記錄。
- 多選欄位預設保留各自原值，使用者明選的 patch 才進候選；混合來源按完整 typed 定義判斷，不能按有效數值合併。
- ordinary／same-face／owner／groups／unknown 每列共同守門，不能篩掉不支援者後提交剩餘部分。
- 候選綁定 selection 順序、工具、face、project context、revision、owner lease 與可見 raw form。晚回覆只可取消自己的 token；確認前重查輸入，雙擊或 detached button 不得重送。
- 正常提交接 ContextHistory；掉回覆保持寫入封鎖，恢復只查原 requestId，不能猜成功或重送。
- 複製／Repeat 只在 session 存在；相同 dump 的 Undo/Redo、切面、重新開檔、重連都使舊 recipe 失效。
- 390px 表單與選單沿既有 shell；輸入框文字剪貼簿、組字中 Enter/Escape 保留。結束組字後 Escape 關自己的編輯表單並返回畫布焦點。

原有單支純欄位修改改走相同 typed candidate，保留未明選來源；實際 anchor／side 修改仍走既有重新定位流程，本片沒有宣稱那條舊流程已改成原地批改。入口、history、requestId、檔案事件及樣式接線在 6/6。

## Unified diff

只擷取下方 diff 區塊內文字，保留最後一個 LF，不含圍欄。此片實際 hunks 掃描為零個反斜線 u 控制字元跳脫，無需還原特定行。

```diff
diff --git a/geometry-web/timber-edit.js b/geometry-web/timber-edit.js
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/geometry-web/timber-edit.js
@@ -0,0 +1,527 @@
+/* H-116-TIMBER/1: one native candidate for ordinary same-face D7 fields.
+   Recipes and unconfirmed edits live only in this module, never project data. */
+(function () {
+  'use strict';
+  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id), esc = value => ReferenceWorkspace.escape(value);
+  const clone = value => structuredClone(value);
+  const canonical = value => JSON.stringify(value, function (_key, item) {
+    return item && !Array.isArray(item) && typeof item === 'object'
+      ? Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(item).sort().map(key => [key,item[key]])) : item;
+  });
+  const roles = [['boundary','邊界材'],['primary','主支'],['secondary','副支'],['custom','自訂']];
+  const directions = ['+u','-u','+v','-v'];
+  let clipboard = null, repeat = null, form = null, menuRead = null, catalogSnapshot = null, closedSelection = null, serial = 0, connectionEpoch = 0;
+  let composing = false, lastConnectionPhase = null;
+  const selectedIds = () => fe.timberSelection.size ? [...fe.timberSelection] : fe.sel?.kind === 'timber' ? [fe.sel.id] : [];
+  function snapshot() {
+    return {context:ReferenceWorkspace.context(), frame:ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame,
+      edit:oc.edit, focus:oc.focus, boundaryId:oc.edit?.bid, placementId:feSid(oc.edit?.bid),
+      session:connection.session, epoch:connectionEpoch, revision:ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().revision,
+      selection:JSON.stringify(selectedIds()), selected:JSON.stringify(fe.sel), tool:fe.tool};
+  }
+  function sameOwner(stamp) {
+    return !!stamp && stamp.context === ReferenceWorkspace.context() && stamp.frame === ReferenceWorkspace.history.frame &&
+      stamp.edit === oc.edit && stamp.focus === oc.focus && oc.focus?.t === 'face' && stamp.boundaryId === oc.edit?.bid &&
+      stamp.placementId === feSid(oc.edit?.bid) && stamp.session === connection.session && stamp.epoch === connectionEpoch;
+  }
+  function current(stamp, selection = true) {
+    return sameOwner(stamp) && stamp.revision === ReferenceWorkspace.getProjectState().revision &&
+      (!selection || selectionCurrent(stamp));
+  }
+  const selectionCurrent = stamp => stamp.selection === JSON.stringify(selectedIds()) && stamp.selected === JSON.stringify(fe.sel) && stamp.tool === fe.tool;
+  const live = value => form === value && current(value.stamp) && !value.closed;
+  function writeReason() {
+    if (connection.phase !== 'ready' || oc.busy) return '請先等候目前操作或重新連線';
+    try { ReferenceWorkspace.assertEditable(); }
+    catch (error) { return error.message; }
+    if (window.RoomCollectionWorkspace?.hasWork || window.RoomOutlineWorkspace?.hasWork) return '請先完成或取消房間草稿';
+    return '';
+  }
+  function revision(value) {
+    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !/^(0|[1-9]\d{0,19})$/.test(value) || BigInt(value) > 18446744073709551615n)
+      throw Error('服務未提供完整的原生版本，請更新服務後重新讀取。');
+    return value;
+  }
+  function priorityText(definition) {
+    const text = definition?.priorityText ?? (Number.isSafeInteger(definition?.priority) && definition.priority > 0 ? String(definition.priority) : null);
+    if (typeof text !== 'string' || !/^[1-9]\d{0,18}$/.test(text) || BigInt(text)>9223372036854775807n)
+      throw Error('優先序缺少完整整數定義，請重新讀取或更新服務。');
+    return text;
+  }
+  async function post(path, body) {
+    const response = await fetch(path, {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','X-Geometry-Version':pageRuntimeVersion},
+      body:JSON.stringify(body),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000)});
+    checkApiSession(response);
+    const result = await response.json();
+    if (!response.ok || !result?.ok) throw Error(result?.error || `角材服務回應失敗（HTTP ${response.status}）。`);
+    return result;
+  }
+  function eligibility(result, ids, boundaryId) {
+    if (result.schema !== 'timber-edit-read-result/1' || result.boundaryId !== boundaryId ||
+        !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(result.contextKey || '') || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.maxOperations) || result.maxOperations < ids.length ||
+        !Array.isArray(result.timbers) || result.timbers.length !== ids.length) return '資格資料不完整，未開放修改或重用';
+    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
+      const row = result.timbers[i];
+      if (!row || row.id !== ids[i] || row.eligible !== true || row.owner !== null || row.groupsKnown !== true ||
+          !Array.isArray(row.groups) || row.groups.length || row.placed !== true || !row.definition?.A || !row.definition?.B ||
+          !row.definition.placement || typeof row.placementOrder !== 'string')
+        return row?.reason || '含所有權、群組或未知資格；整筆不能修改或重用';
+      priorityText(row.definition);
+    }
+    return result.eligible === true ? '' : result.reason || '這組角材不符合普通已放置角材資格';
+  }
+  async function read(ids, stamp, selection = true) {
+    if (!ids.length) throw Error('請先選取同面角材。');
+    const result = await post('/timber-edit/read', {schema:'timber-edit-read/1',boundaryId:stamp.boundaryId,timberIds:ids});

+  function createHTML(value) {

+      `<details open><summary>來自 ${esc(recipe.label)} · 定義與來源</summary><div>A：${dimensionHTML(definition.A,recipe.effective.A)}</div><div>B：${dimensionHTML(definition.B,recipe.effective.B)}</div><p>角色 ${esc(definition.role)} · 優先序 ${esc(priorityText(definition))}</p></details>` +

+  function report(value, message, error = false) {

+  function updateActions(value) {

+          !Array.isArray(candidate.before) || !Array.isArray(candidate.after) || !Array.isArray(candidate.violations) ||
+          !candidate.noOp && (!Number.isInteger(candidate.token) || candidate.token<1 || candidate.token>4294967295)) throw Error('候選資料不完整，未開放確認。');
+      value.preview = candidate; value.pending = null;
+      if (!live(value) || generation !== value.generation || input !== rawForm()) {await cancelCandidate(value);return;}
+      if (candidate.noOp) {
+        if (candidate.token !== null) throw Error('空變更候選不應具有確認識別。');

+      } else ReferenceWorkspace.bindTimberPreview(value.lease,candidate.token);
+      value.previewInput = input;impact(value);

+    } catch (error) {
+      value.pending = null;
+      await cancelCandidate(value).catch(()=>{});
+      if (live(value)) report(value,error.message,true);
+    } finally {value.pending=null;value.busy=false;if(live(value))updateActions(value);}
+  }
+  async function confirm(value) {
+    if (!live(value) || value.busy || !value.preview?.valid || value.preview.noOp) return;
+    if (value.previewInput !== rawForm()) {changed(value);throw Error('輸入已變更，請重新預覽。');}
+    ReferenceWorkspace.assertTimberPreview(value.lease,value.preview.token);
+    value.busy=true;value.confirming=true;updateActions(value);
+    const token=value.preview.token,result=await apiRequest('timber-edit-confirm '+token);
+    if (result.transport) {value.unknown=true;report(value,'確認結果尚未取得；重新連線只查這次回執，不會重送。',true);return;}
+    value.confirming=false;value.busy=false;value.lease=null;value.preview=null;
+    if (!result.ok) {if(form===value){report(value,result.error || '候選已失效，請重新選取。',true);updateActions(value);}return;}
+    await settled(value,result);
+  }
+  async function settled(value,result) {
+    clipboard=null;repeat=null;menuRead=null;
+    const owns=form===value && sameOwner(value.stamp) && selectionCurrent(value.stamp);
+    form=null;value.closed=true;
+    if (!owns) return;
+    await feAfterChange();
+    const created=result.created?.find(item=>item.handle==='new:0' && item.kind==='timber');
+    if (created && Number.isSafeInteger(created.id)) await recordCreated(created.id,value.stamp.boundaryId);
+    toast('角材變更已一次套用，可復原整筆操作。');
+  }
+  async function recordCreated(id,boundaryId) {
+    const stamp=snapshot();if(stamp.boundaryId!==boundaryId || connection.phase!=='ready')return;

+    }
+    catch (_) {repeat=null;}
+  }
+  function close(focus) {
+    const value=form;if(!value)return;
+    if(value.confirming){report(value,'確認結果尚未取得；請等候或重新連線查回執。',true);return;}
+    if(focus)closedSelection=snapshot();
+    form=null;value.closed=true;value.generation++;
+    cancelCandidate(value).catch(error=>toast(error.message));

+  }

+    if (form && !form.confirming) {const value=form;close(false);report(value,reason,true);}
+  }
+  function connectionChanged() {

+  }
+  async function beforeWorldMutation() {

+    form=null;value.closed=true;value.generation++;
+    await cancelCandidate(value);
+  }
+  async function settleRecovered(command,result) {
+    if(!command.startsWith('timber-edit-confirm '))return;

+    if(!value?.unknown)return;

+    if(result.ok)await settled(value,result);

+  }
+  async function applyLegacyFields(id) {
+    const stamp=snapshot(), ids=selectedIds();if(ids.length!==1||ids[0]!==id)return false;
+    const input=Object.fromEntries(['A','B','Dir','Side','Role','Pri','Anchor','Selector'].map(key=>[key,$('tbEdit'+key)?.value ?? '']));
+    const priorityEdited=input.Pri!==$('tbEditPri')?.getAttribute('value');
+    try {
+      const result=await read(ids,stamp);if(!result.eligible)throw Error(result.reason);
+      const old=result.timbers[0],placement=old.definition.placement;
+      if(!current(stamp) || Object.entries(input).some(([key,text])=>$('tbEdit'+key)?.value!==text))throw Error('輸入已變更，請重新核對。');
+      if(input.Anchor!==placement.anchor || input.Selector.trim()!==placement.selector || input.Side!==placement.side)return false;
+      const value=start('batch');value.read=result;renderBatch(value);
+      const set=(field,id,text)=>{document.querySelector(`[data-timber-patch="${field}"]`).checked=true;$(id).value=text;};
+      for(const field of ['A','B'])if(Number(input[field])!==old.effective[field]){set(field,'timberEdit'+field+'Mode','direct');$('timberEdit'+field+'Value').value=input[field];}
+      if(input.Dir!==placement.direction)set('direction','timberEditDirection',input.Dir);
+      if(input.Role!==old.definition.role)set('role','timberEditRole',input.Role);
+      if(input.Role==='custom' && (input.Role!==old.definition.role || priorityEdited && priorityText({priorityText:input.Pri.trim()})!==priorityText(old.definition)))set('priority','timberEditPriority',input.Pri);

+    } catch(error){toast(error.message);return true;}
+  }
+  const safe=async action=>{try{await action();}catch(error){toast(error.message);if(form)report(form,error.message,true);}};
+  document.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>{composing=true;},true);
+  document.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>{composing=false;},true);
+  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
+    if(event.isComposing || composing || event.keyCode===229){

+    }

+    if(!(event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey) || event.altKey || !['c','v','d'].includes(key))return;

 - **範圍**：#116 B 的其餘——物件畫布、空間平面圖與三視圖的游標 v2／右鍵選單；複製／貼上／重複與多選批改；觸控長按、偏移準星、雙指（PR #125 明示未做）。
 - **GEO-219 🔒 減項要求**：**新增入口須寫明減項**——每個子項若新增可見入口、按鈕或常駐說明，提案與工單都必須寫明本次拿掉或合併了什麼；寫不出減項就先入決策佇列，不得只加不減；每個工作畫面常駐操作說明不超過 3 塊（AGENTS.md「UI 減重紀律」，Q21）。先例：PR #125 以 B 動作卡取代原右鍵純動作清單，右鍵開啟時收起重複浮動操作列，停用理由併入選單既有 footer。PR #352（K4 PR-c 第一片／R11）的本次減項：移除物件畫布工具列的 `#homeButton` 與舊 listener，回到原點統一由物件畫布右鍵／鍵盤選單呼叫原 `ocHome`；停用理由沿用選單 footer，不新增常駐說明。
+- **R11 第二片 A／#116 本次減項（2026-10-29）**：面內選單原兩列停用的「複製／貼上」合成一個「重用角材」子選單；多選右欄原要求改成單選的操作區，換成同一角材欄位表單，原選取提示改為指向此表單。單支與多支欄位修改共用 typed patch、完整候選與一次確認；原定位／side 修改入口保留。來源、混合值、拒絕原因與連動核對併入表單原回覆區及選單 footer，不新增視窗、全域工具列或常駐教學塊；不重算第一片的 homeButton 減項。範圍僅同專案同面普通已放置 D7，任意子圖、跨面／跨專案與群組修改仍未實作；實測與未驗項以本片補丁回報為準。
 - **驗收**：待提案定案；實體裝置（觸控）歸 GEO-250。
 
 ### GEO-255 — PF-1～PF-6 面材鋪滿實作（2026-10-15，PF-0／PF-1／PF-2 已落地；後續未完成；決策 F）

+/* R11: the existing inspector owns both single and multiple timber field edits. */
+.timber-edit { min-width: 0; }
+.timber-edit fieldset { min-width: 0; margin: 10px 0; padding: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; }

+.timber-edit input[type="checkbox"] { width: auto; flex: none; }
+.timber-edit-controls, .timber-edit .place-form { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr); gap: 8px; }
+.timber-edit-controls label { display: grid; gap: 4px; }
+.timber-edit input, .timber-edit select { box-sizing: border-box; width: 100%; min-width: 0; max-width: 100%; }

+.timber-edit-definition { padding: 6px 0; }
+.timber-edit-definition > div { margin: 4px 0; }
+.timber-edit-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px; }

+.timber-edit [hidden] { display: none !important; }
